Mickey Mania (E) -- Krisalis sound data (driver revision 3)
==============================================================================
Music module at ROM $1D8000:
  +$000  8 x (offset.w, length.w)  sample table (offset within ROM bank $3A = $1D0000-$1D7FFF)
  +$020  order format flag = $FFFF -> WORD order entries (12 bytes per order row)
  +$022  pattern pointer table: 511 longs (498 distinct)
  $1D881E order list: 237 rows x 6 columns (FM1..FM6)
  $1D933A-$1DFE05 pattern data
  $1DFE06 voice bank (64 x 32 bytes uploaded to Z80 $0400)
Level music table $0184FA (words): 2, 130, 130, 226, 151, 144, 129, 74, 85, 74, 92, 106

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PCM samples (signed 8-bit; the Z80 adds $80).  Rate byte r from the pattern cell;
approximate playback rate on the idle loop: r/256 x Fz80/(112+72r/256)
Revision 3 skips the first 8 and the last 4 bytes of every sample.
